Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 191-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
191 |   @Override
192 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
193 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
194 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
195 |     // show up in the type registry.
196 |     Scope newScope = null;
197 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
198 |     if (parent == null) {
199 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
200 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
201 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
202 | 
203 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
204 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
205 | 
206 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
207 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
208 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
209 |     } else {
210 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
211 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
212 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
213 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
217 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
218 | 
219 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
220 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
221 |     // build properties on.
222 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
223 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
224 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
225 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
226 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
227 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
228 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
229 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
230 |         }
231 |       }
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     if (parent == null) {
235 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
236 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
237 |           delegateCallingConventions);
238 |     }
239 |     return newScope;
240 |   }

Method code:
449 |     void resolveTypes() {
450 |       // Resolve types and attach them to nodes.
451 |       for (DeferredSetType deferred : deferredSetTypes) {
452 |         deferred.resolve(scope);
453 |       }
454 | 
455 |       // Resolve types and attach them to scope slots.
456 |       Iterator<Var> vars = scope.getVars();
457 |       while (vars.hasNext()) {
458 |         vars.next().resolveType(typeParsingErrorReporter);
459 |       }
460 | 
461 |       // Tell the type registry that any remaining types
462 |       // are unknown.
463 |       typeRegistry.resolveTypesInScope(scope);
464 |     }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1262-1310
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1262 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1263 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1264 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1265 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1266 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1267 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1268 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1269 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1270 |       } else if (info != null) {
1271 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1272 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1273 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1274 |           } else {
1275 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1276 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1277 |           }
1278 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1279 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1280 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1281 |         } else {
1282 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1283 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1284 |             JSType knownType = null;
1285 |             if (rValue != null) {
1286 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1287 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1288 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1289 |               } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1290 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1291 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1292 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1293 |                 // reasons.
1294 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1295 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1296 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1297 |                     && lValue.isName()
1298 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1299 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1300 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1301 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1302 |                 }
1303 |               }
1304 |             }
1305 |           }
1306 |         }
1307 |       }
1308 | 
1309 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1310 |     }

===== M0027 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 844-945
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the jsdoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
844 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
845 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
846 |         @Nullable String name,
847 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
848 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
849 | 
850 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
851 | 
852 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
853 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
854 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
855 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
856 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
857 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
858 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
859 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
860 |             functionType = aliasedType;
861 | 
862 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
863 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
864 |             }
865 |           }
866 |         }
867 |       }
868 | 
869 |       if (functionType == null) {
870 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
871 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
872 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
873 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
874 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
875 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
876 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
877 | 
878 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
879 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
880 | 
881 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
882 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
883 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
884 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
885 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
886 |           }
887 |         }
888 | 
889 |         if (functionType == null) {
890 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
891 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
892 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
893 |           Var ownerVar = null;
894 |           String propName = null;
895 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
896 |           if (ownerName != null) {
897 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
898 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
899 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
900 |             }
901 |             if (name != null) {
902 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
903 |             }
904 |           }
905 | 
906 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
907 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
908 |             overriddenPropType =
909 |                 findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
910 |           }
911 | 
912 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
913 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
914 |                   scope)
915 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
916 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
917 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
918 |               .inferReturnType(info)
919 |               .inferInheritance(info);
920 | 
921 | 
922 |           // Infer the context type.
923 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
924 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
925 |             builder.inferThisType(
926 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
927 |             searchedForThisType = true;
928 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
929 |             builder.inferThisType(info, ownerNode.getJSType());
930 |             searchedForThisType = true;
931 |           }
932 | 
933 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
934 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
935 |           }
936 | 
937 |           functionType = builder
938 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
939 |               .buildAndRegister();
940 |         }
941 |       }
942 | 
943 |       // all done
944 |       return functionType;
945 |     }

===== M0038 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1659-1700
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1659 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1660 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1661 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1662 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1663 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1664 |       // {?} - right curly
1665 |       // {?=} - equals
1666 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1667 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1668 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1669 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1670 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1671 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1672 |       token = next();
1673 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1674 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1675 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1676 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1677 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1678 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1679 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1680 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1681 |       }
1682 | 
1683 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1684 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1685 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1686 |     } else {
1687 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1688 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1689 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1690 |           next();
1691 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1692 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1693 |           next();
1694 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1695 |         }
1696 |       }
1697 | 
1698 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1699 |     }
1700 |   }

===== M0040 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 511-556
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
512 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
513 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
514 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
515 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
516 |     if (objectType == null) {
517 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
518 |     } else {
519 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
520 |         return;
521 |       }
522 | 
523 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
524 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
525 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
526 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
527 |         //
528 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
529 |         //    inferred type.
530 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
531 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
532 |         //    define it.
533 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
534 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
535 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
536 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
537 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
538 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
539 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
540 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
541 |           } else {
542 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
543 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
544 |           }
545 |         } else {
546 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().isThis() &&
547 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
548 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
549 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
550 |           } else {
551 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
552 |           }
553 |         }
554 |       }
555 |     }
556 |   }

/**
   * Gets the number of properties of this object.
   */
Method code:
151 |   @Override
152 |   public int getPropertiesCount() {
153 |     ObjectType implicitPrototype = getImplicitPrototype();
154 |     if (implicitPrototype == null) {
155 |       return this.properties.size();
156 |     }
157 |     int localCount = 0;
158 |     for (String property : properties.keySet()) {
159 |       if (!implicitPrototype.hasProperty(property)) {
160 |         localCount++;
161 |       }
162 |     }
163 |     return implicitPrototype.getPropertiesCount() + localCount;
164 |   }

   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not

===== M0052 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 398-411
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
398 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
399 |       Node ret;
400 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
401 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
402 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
403 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
404 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
405 |       } else {
406 |         ret = transform(n);
407 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
408 |       }
409 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
410 |       return ret;
411 |     }

===== M0067 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 365-408
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
365 |   boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
366 |     if (prototype == null) {
367 |       return false;
368 |     }
369 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
370 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
371 |       return false;
372 |     }
373 | 
374 |     PrototypeObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
375 |         ? null : (PrototypeObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
376 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
377 | 
378 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
379 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
380 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
381 | 
382 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
383 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
384 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
385 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
386 |     }
387 | 
388 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
389 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
390 |       if (superClass != null) {
391 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       if (isInterface()) {
395 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
396 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
397 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
398 |           }
399 |         }
400 |       }
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
404 |       clearCachedValues();
405 |     }
406 | 
407 |     return true;
408 |   }

===== M0068 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2021-2033
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as synactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2021 |   static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2022 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2023 |         n.isGetElem());
2024 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2025 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2026 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2027 |         || parent.isVar()
2028 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2029 |         || parent.isDec()
2030 |         || parent.isInc()
2031 |         || parent.isParamList()
2032 |         || parent.isCatch();
2033 |   }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 177-200
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
177 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
178 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
179 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
180 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
181 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
182 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
183 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
184 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
185 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
186 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
187 |           return false;
188 |         }
189 |       } else {
190 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
191 |           return false;
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
195 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
196 |     }
197 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both

===== M0096 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

466 |     @Override
467 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
468 |         Node parent) {
469 |       inputId = t.getInputId();

471 |           n.isScript()) {

479 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();

493 |       }
494 |

496 |     }

===== M0116 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1671-1690
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the condition of an ON_TRUE / ON_FALSE CFG edge.
   * @param n a node with an outgoing conditional CFG edge
   * @return the condition node or null if the condition is not obviously a node
   */
Method code:
1671 |   static Node getConditionExpression(Node n) {
1672 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1673 |       case Token.IF:
1674 |       case Token.WHILE:
1675 |         return n.getFirstChild();
1676 |       case Token.DO:
1677 |         return n.getLastChild();
1678 |       case Token.FOR:
1679 |         switch (n.getChildCount()) {
1680 |           case 3:
1681 |             return null;
1682 |           case 4:
1683 |             return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
1684 |         }
1685 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("malformed 'for' statement " + n);
1686 |       case Token.CASE:
1687 |         return null;
1688 |     }
1689 |     throw new IllegalArgumentException(n + " does not have a condition.");
1690 |   }

1160 |       sourcePosition = mergeLineCharNo(getLineno(), charno);

===== M0131 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 690-787
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
690 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
691 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
692 |     /*
693 |      * This is the case where:
694 |      *
695 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
696 |      * the script.
697 |      *
698 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
699 |      * the caller of the function.
700 |      *
701 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
702 |      * back to the caller of the function.
703 |      *
704 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
705 |      * asked to traverse.
706 |      *
707 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
708 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
709 |      */
710 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
711 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
712 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
713 |       return null;
714 |     }
715 | 
716 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
717 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
718 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
719 |       case Token.IF:
720 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
723 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
724 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
725 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
726 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
727 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
728 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
730 |           } else {
731 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
732 |           }
733 |         } else {
734 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
735 |         }
736 |         break;
737 |       case Token.FOR:
738 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
739 |           return parent;
740 |         } else {
741 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
742 |         }
743 |       case Token.WHILE:
744 |       case Token.DO:
745 |         return parent;
746 |       case Token.TRY:
747 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
748 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
749 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
750 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
751 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
752 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
753 |           }
754 |         // CATCH block.
755 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
756 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
757 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
758 |           } else {
759 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
760 |           }
761 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
762 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
763 |           if (cfa != null) {
764 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
765 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
766 |             }
767 |           }
768 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
769 |         }
770 |     }
771 | 
772 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
773 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
774 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
775 | 
776 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
777 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
778 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
779 |     }
780 | 
781 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
782 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
783 |     } else {
784 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
785 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
786 |     }
787 |   }

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 331-358
Documentation:

Method code:
331 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
332 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
333 |     // two cases:
334 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
335 |     // and
336 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
337 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
338 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
339 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
340 |     //
341 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
342 |     //
343 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
344 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
345 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
346 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
347 |     //
348 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
349 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
350 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
351 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
352 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
353 | 
354 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
355 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
356 |     }
357 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType, propertyNode);
358 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.

===== M0145 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node,FlowScope,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 123-263
Documentation:

Method code:
123 |   @Override
124 |   public FlowScope getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node condition,
125 |       FlowScope blindScope, boolean outcome) {
126 |     // Check for the typeof operator.
127 |     int operatorToken = condition.getType();
128 |     switch (operatorToken) {
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.NE:
131 |       case Token.SHEQ:
132 |       case Token.SHNE:
133 |       case Token.CASE:
134 |         Node left;
135 |         Node right;
136 |         if (operatorToken == Token.CASE) {
137 |           left = condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
138 |           right = condition.getFirstChild();
139 |         } else {
140 |           left = condition.getFirstChild();
141 |           right = condition.getLastChild();
142 |         }
143 | 
144 |         Node typeOfNode = null;
145 |         Node stringNode = null;
146 |         if (left.isTypeOf() && right.isString()) {
147 |           typeOfNode = left;
148 |           stringNode = right;
149 |         } else if (right.isTypeOf() &&
150 |                    left.isString()) {
151 |           typeOfNode = right;
152 |           stringNode = left;
153 |         }
154 |         if (typeOfNode != null && stringNode != null) {
155 |           Node operandNode = typeOfNode.getFirstChild();
156 |           JSType operandType = getTypeIfRefinable(operandNode, blindScope);
157 |           if (operandType != null) {
158 |             boolean resultEqualsValue = operatorToken == Token.EQ ||
159 |                 operatorToken == Token.SHEQ || operatorToken == Token.CASE;
160 |             if (!outcome) {
161 |               resultEqualsValue = !resultEqualsValue;
162 |             }
163 |             return caseTypeOf(operandNode, operandType, stringNode.getString(),
164 |                 resultEqualsValue, blindScope);
165 |           }
166 |         }
167 |     }
168 |     switch (operatorToken) {
169 |       case Token.AND:
170 |         if (outcome) {
171 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
172 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
173 |         } else {
174 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
175 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
176 |         }
177 | 
178 |       case Token.OR:
179 |         if (!outcome) {
180 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
181 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
182 |         } else {
183 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
184 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
185 |         }
186 | 
187 |       case Token.EQ:
188 |         if (outcome) {
189 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
190 |         } else {
191 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
192 |         }
193 | 
194 |       case Token.NE:
195 |         if (outcome) {
196 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
197 |         } else {
198 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |       case Token.SHEQ:
202 |         if (outcome) {
203 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
204 |         } else {
205 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
206 |         }
207 | 
208 |       case Token.SHNE:
209 |         if (outcome) {
210 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
211 |         } else {
212 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
213 |         }
214 | 
215 |       case Token.NAME:
216 |       case Token.GETPROP:
217 |         return caseNameOrGetProp(condition, blindScope, outcome);
218 | 
219 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
220 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
221 |             condition.getFirstChild(),
222 |             firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
223 |                 condition.getFirstChild().getNext(), blindScope, outcome),
224 |             outcome);
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NOT:
227 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
228 |             condition.getFirstChild(), blindScope, !outcome);
229 | 
230 |       case Token.LE:
231 |       case Token.LT:
232 |       case Token.GE:
233 |       case Token.GT:
234 |         if (outcome) {
235 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, INEQ);
236 |         }
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
240 |         return caseInstanceOf(
241 |             condition.getFirstChild(), condition.getLastChild(), blindScope,
242 |             outcome);
243 | 
244 |       case Token.IN:
245 |         if (outcome && condition.getFirstChild().isString()) {
246 |           return caseIn(condition.getLastChild(),
247 |               condition.getFirstChild().getString(), blindScope);
248 |         }
249 |         break;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.CASE:
252 |         Node left =
253 |             condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
254 |         Node right = condition.getFirstChild();
255 |         if (outcome) {
256 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHEQ);
257 |         } else {
258 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHNE);
259 |         }
260 |     }
261 |     return nextPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
262 |         condition, blindScope, outcome);
263 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processName(Name,boolean)

Method code:
742 |     Node processName(Name nameNode, boolean asString) {

744 |         return newStringNode(Token.STRING, nameNode.getIdentifier());

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

===== M0164 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 323-370
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
323 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
324 |     if (info != null) {
325 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
326 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
327 | 
328 |       // base type
329 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
330 |         if (isConstructor) {
331 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
332 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
333 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
334 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
335 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
336 |           }
337 |         } else {
338 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
339 |         }
340 |       }
341 | 
342 |       // implemented interfaces
343 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
344 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
345 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
346 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
347 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
348 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
349 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
350 |           }
351 |         }
352 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
353 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
354 |       }
355 | 
356 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
357 |       if (isInterface) {
358 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
359 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
360 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
361 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
362 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
363 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
364 |           }
365 |         }
366 |       }
367 |     }
368 | 
369 |     return this;
370 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.resolve(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)

Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
1078 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1079 |     if (resolved) {
1080 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
1081 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
1082 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
1083 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1084 |       }
1085 |       return resolveResult;
1086 |     }
1087 |     resolved = true;
1088 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
1089 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
1090 |     return resolveResult;
1091 |   }

Original range: 908-929
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
908 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
909 |     switch (n.getType()) {
910 |       case Token.CALL:
911 |       case Token.GETPROP:
912 |       case Token.GETELEM:
913 |       case Token.THROW:
914 |       case Token.NEW:
915 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
916 |       case Token.INC:
917 |       case Token.DEC:
918 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
919 |         return true;
920 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
921 |         return false;
922 |     }
923 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
924 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
925 |         return true;
926 |       }
927 |     }
928 |     return false;
929 |   }

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 661-697
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
661 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
662 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
663 |         fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), parametersNode, returnType);
664 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
665 | 
666 |     if (existingType != null) {
667 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType.isInstanceType();
668 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
669 |         FunctionType existingFn =
670 |             isInstanceObject ?
671 |             existingType.toObjectType().getConstructor() :
672 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
673 | 
674 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
675 |           existingFn.setSource(contents.getSourceNode());
676 |         }
677 | 
678 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
679 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
680 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
681 |         }
682 | 
683 |         return existingFn;
684 |       } else {
685 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
686 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
687 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
688 |       }
689 |     }
690 | 
691 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
692 | 
693 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
694 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
695 |     }
696 |     return fnType;
697 |   }

===== M0199 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1634-1664
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1634 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1635 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1636 |         Node n = stub.node;
1637 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1638 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1639 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1640 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1641 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1642 | 
1643 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1644 |           continue;
1645 |         }
1646 | 
1647 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1648 |         // in the type registry.
1649 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1650 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1651 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1652 | 
1653 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1654 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1655 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1656 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1657 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1658 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1659 |         } else {
1660 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1661 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1662 |         }
1663 |       }
1664 |     }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 609-677
Documentation:

Method code:
609 |     @Override
610 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
611 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
612 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
613 |       if (name == null) {
614 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
615 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
616 |           errorReporter.error(
617 |             "unnamed function statement",
618 |             sourceName,
619 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
620 | 
621 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
622 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
623 |         }
624 |         name = new Name();
625 |         name.setIdentifier("");
626 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
627 |       }
628 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
629 |       Node newName = transform(name);
630 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
631 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
632 |         // declaration.
633 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
634 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
635 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
636 |         // same line as the function keyword.
637 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
638 |             functionNode.getLp();
639 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
640 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
641 |       }
642 | 
643 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
644 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
645 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
646 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
647 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
648 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
649 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
650 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
651 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
652 |       if (fnName != null) {
653 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
654 |       } else {
655 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
656 |       }
657 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
658 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
659 | 
660 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
661 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
662 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
663 |       }
664 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
665 | 
666 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
667 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
668 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
669 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
670 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
671 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
672 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
673 |       }
674 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
675 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
676 |      return node;
677 |     }

/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
683 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {

685 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
686 |       return that.toMaybeUnionType().getLeastSupertype(this);
687 |     }
688 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);
689 |   }

===== M0218 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1113-1137
Documentation:
/**
   * Suppose X is an object with inferred properties.
   * Suppose also that X is used in a way where it would only type-check
   * correctly if some of those properties are widened.
   * Then we should be polite and automatically widen X's properties for him.
   *
   * For a concrete example, consider:
   * param x {{prop: (number|undefined)}}
   * function f(x) {}
   * f({});
   *
   * If we give the anonymous object an inferred property of (number|undefined),
   * then this code will type-check appropriately.
   */
Method code:
1113 |   private void inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(
1114 |       JSType type, JSType constraint) {
1115 |     if (type == null || constraint == null) {
1116 |       return;
1117 |     }
1118 | 
1119 |     ObjectType constraintObj =
1120 |         ObjectType.cast(constraint.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1121 |     if (constraintObj != null && constraintObj.isRecordType()) {
1122 |       ObjectType objType = ObjectType.cast(type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1123 |       if (objType != null) {
1124 |         for (String prop : constraintObj.getOwnPropertyNames()) {
1125 |           JSType propType = constraintObj.getPropertyType(prop);
1126 |           if (!objType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(prop)) {
1127 |             JSType typeToInfer = propType;
1128 |             if (!objType.hasProperty(prop)) {
1129 |               typeToInfer =
1130 |                   getNativeType(VOID_TYPE).getLeastSupertype(propType);
1131 |             }
1132 |             objType.defineInferredProperty(prop, typeToInfer, null);
1133 |           }
1134 |         }
1135 |       }
1136 |     }
1137 |   }

===== M0219 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 463-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
463 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
464 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
465 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
466 |     switch (left.getType()) {
467 |       case Token.NAME:
468 |         String varName = left.getString();
469 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
470 | 
471 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
472 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
473 |         // This has two purposes:
474 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
475 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
476 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
477 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
478 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
479 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
480 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
481 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
482 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
483 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
484 |         }
485 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
486 |             resultType : null);
487 | 
488 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
489 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
490 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
491 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
492 |         }
493 |         break;
494 |       case Token.GETPROP:
495 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
496 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
497 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
498 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
499 |               resultType);
500 |         }
501 | 
502 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
503 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
504 |         break;
505 |     }
506 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 459-474
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
459 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
460 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
461 |       Set<String> directives = null;
462 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
463 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
464 |         if (directives == null) {
465 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
466 |         } else {
467 |           directives.add(directive);
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       if (directives != null) {
472 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
473 |       }
474 |     }

Original range: 898-910
Documentation:
/**
   * Looks up a type by name. To allow for forward references to types, an
   * unrecognized string has to be bound to a NamedType object that will be
   * resolved later.
   *
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name resolution.
   * @param jsTypeName The name string.
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file where this reference appears.
   * @param lineno The line number of the reference.
   * @return a NamedType if the string argument is not one of the known types,
   *     otherwise the corresponding JSType object.
   */
Method code:
898 |   public JSType getType(StaticScope<JSType> scope, String jsTypeName,
899 |       String sourceName, int lineno, int charno) {
900 |     JSType type = getType(jsTypeName);
901 |     if (type == null) {
902 |       // TODO(user): Each instance should support named type creation using
903 |       // interning.
904 |       NamedType namedType =
905 |           new NamedType(this, jsTypeName, sourceName, lineno, charno);
906 |       unresolvedNamedTypes.put(scope, namedType);

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.FlatFlowScopeCache.FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope,LinkedFlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 416-480
Documentation:
// A cache at the join of two scope chains.
Method code:
416 |     FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeA,
417 |         LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeB) {
418 |       linkedEquivalent = null;
419 | 
420 |       // Always prefer the "real" function scope to the faked-out
421 |       // bottom scope.
422 |       functionScope = joinedScopeA.flowsFromBottom() ?
423 |           joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope() : joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope();
424 | 
425 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsA = joinedScopeA.allFlowSlots();
426 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsB = joinedScopeB.allFlowSlots();
427 | 
428 |       symbols = slotsA;
429 | 
430 |       // There are 5 different join cases:
431 |       // 1) The type is declared in joinedScopeA, not in joinedScopeB,
432 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in A.
433 |       // 2) The type is declared in joinedScopeB, not in joinedScopeA,
434 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in B.
435 |       // 3) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeA, but
436 |       //    not in joinedScopeB. Join the two types.
437 |       // 4) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeB, but
438 |       //    not in joinedScopeA. Join the two types.
439 |       // 5) The type is declared in joinedScopeA and joinedScopeB. Join
440 |       //    the two types.
441 |       Set<String> symbolNames = Sets.newHashSet(symbols.keySet());
442 |       symbolNames.addAll(slotsB.keySet());
443 | 
444 |       for (String name : symbolNames) {
445 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotA = slotsA.get(name);
446 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotB = slotsB.get(name);
447 | 
448 |         JSType joinedType = null;
449 |         if (slotB == null || slotB.getType() == null) {
450 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
451 |               = joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
452 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
453 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
454 |             // Case #1 -- already inserted.
455 |           } else {
456 |             // Case #3
457 |             joinedType = slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
458 |           }
459 |         } else if (slotA == null || slotA.getType() == null) {
460 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
461 |               = joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
462 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
463 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
464 |             // Case #2
465 |             symbols.put(name, slotB);
466 |           } else {
467 |             // Case #4
468 |             joinedType = slotB.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
469 |           }
470 |         } else {
471 |           // Case #5
472 |           joinedType =
473 |               slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(slotB.getType());
474 |         }
475 | 
476 |         if (joinedType != null) {
477 |           symbols.put(name, new SimpleSlot(name, joinedType, true));
478 |         }
479 |       }
480 |     }

===== M0264 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1182-1190
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1182 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1183 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1184 |       return -1;
1185 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1186 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1187 |     } else {
1188 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1189 |     }
1190 |   }

===== M0269 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyAccess(JSType,String,NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1291-1318
Documentation:
/**
   * Emit a warning if we can prove that a property cannot possibly be
   * defined on an object. Note the difference between JS and a strictly
   * statically typed language: we're checking if the property
   * *cannot be defined*, whereas a java compiler would check if the
   * property *can be undefined*.
   */
Method code:
1291 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1292 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1293 |     // If the property type is unknown, check the object type to see if it
1294 |     // can ever be defined. We explicitly exclude CHECKED_UNKNOWN (for
1295 |     // properties where we've checked that it exists, or for properties on
1296 |     // objects that aren't in this binary).
1297 |     JSType propType = getJSType(n);
1298 |     if (propType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1299 |       childType = childType.autobox();
1300 |       ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(childType);
1301 |       if (objectType != null) {
1302 |         // We special-case object types so that checks on enums can be
1303 |         // much stricter, and so that we can use hasProperty (which is much
1304 |         // faster in most cases).
1305 |         if (!objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
1306 |             objectType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1307 |           if (objectType instanceof EnumType) {
1308 |             report(t, n, INEXISTENT_ENUM_ELEMENT, propName);
1309 |           } else {
1310 |             checkPropertyAccessHelper(objectType, propName, t, n);
1311 |           }
1312 |         }
1313 | 
1314 |       } else {
1315 |         checkPropertyAccessHelper(childType, propName, t, n);
1316 |       }
1317 |     }
1318 |   }

/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1610 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1611 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1612 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1613 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1614 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {

1617 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1618 |           next();
1619 |         }
1620 |         skipEOLs();
1621 |         token = next();
1622 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1623 |       }
1624 |     }
1625 |     return typeExpr;
1626 |   }

156 |   @Override
157 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType that) {
158 |     if (this == that) {
159 |       return true;
160 |     } else if (this.isNominalType()) {
161 |       ObjectType thatObj = ObjectType.cast(that);

164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |     return false;
167 |   }

===== M0320 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 778-804
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infimums, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
778 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
779 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
780 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
781 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
782 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
783 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
784 |       UnionType unionType = type.toMaybeUnionType();
785 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
786 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
787 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
788 |           needsFiltering = true;
789 |           break;
790 |         }
791 |       }
792 | 
793 |       if (needsFiltering) {
794 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
795 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
796 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
797 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
798 |           }
799 |         }
800 |         return builder.build();
801 |       }
802 |     }
803 |     return type;
804 |   }

1026 |   public void setSource(Node source) {

1028 |       // NOTE(bashir): On one hand when source is null we want to drop any
1029 |       // references to old nodes retained in prototypeSlot. On the other hand

===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1133-1155
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor or an interface type, find out whether the unknown
   * type is a supertype of the current type.
   */
Method code:
1133 |   private static boolean hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(FunctionType ctor) {
1134 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1135 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!ctor.isUnknownType());
1136 | 
1137 |     // The type system should notice inheritance cycles on its own
1138 |     // and break the cycle.
1139 |     while (true) {
1140 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
1141 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
1142 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
1143 |         return false;
1144 |       }
1145 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType() ||
1146 |           maybeSuperInstanceType.isEmptyType()) {
1147 |         return true;
1148 |       }
1149 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
1150 |       if (ctor == null) {
1151 |         return false;
1152 |       }
1153 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1154 |     }
1155 |   }

===== M0327 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.

344 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
345 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
346 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work ok.

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0339 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M0348 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1840-1864
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1840 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1841 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1842 |     Preconditions.checkState(!n.isFunction() ||
1843 |             type.isFunctionType() ||
1844 |             type.isUnknownType());
1845 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1846 |     if (info != null) {
1847 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1848 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1849 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1850 |         type = infoType;
1851 |       }
1852 | 
1853 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1854 |         String propName = n.isGetProp() ?
1855 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1856 |         compiler.report(
1857 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1858 |       }
1859 |     }
1860 | 
1861 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1862 |       n.setJSType(type);
1863 |     }
1864 |   }
