===== M0353 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 498-555
Documentation:

Method code:
498 |     @Override
499 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
500 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
501 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
502 | 
503 |       switch (n.getType()) {
504 |         case Token.CALL:
505 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
506 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
507 |           break;
508 | 
509 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
510 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
511 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
512 |           }
513 | 
514 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
515 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
516 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 | 
520 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
521 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
522 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
523 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
524 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
525 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
526 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
527 |           }
528 |           break;
529 | 
530 |         case Token.CATCH:
531 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
532 |           break;
533 | 
534 |         case Token.VAR:
535 |           defineVar(n, parent);
536 |           break;
537 | 
538 |         case Token.GETPROP:
539 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
540 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
541 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
542 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
543 |           }
544 |           break;
545 |       }
546 | 
547 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
548 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
549 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
550 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
551 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
552 |         }
553 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
554 |       }
555 |     }

===== M0383 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 556-586
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
556 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
557 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
558 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
559 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
560 |     if (isOptional) {
561 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
562 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
563 |       // error can be reported.
564 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
565 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
566 |         emittedWarning = true;
567 |       }
568 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
569 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
570 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
571 |         emittedWarning = true;
572 |       }
573 |     } else {
574 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
575 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
576 |         // or var arg so it is an error.

===== M0386 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitObjLitKey(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 943-1004
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an object literal field definition <code>key : value</code>.
   *
   * If the <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the
   * schema of the object type it is referring to.
   *
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param key the assign node
   */
Method code:
943 |   private void visitObjLitKey(NodeTraversal t, Node key, Node objlit) {
944 |     // Do not validate object lit value types in externs. We don't really care,
945 |     // and it makes it easier to generate externs.
946 |     if (objlit.isFromExterns()) {
947 |       ensureTyped(t, key);
948 |       return;
949 |     }
950 | 
951 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Validate get and set function declarations are valid
952 |     // as is the functions can have "extraneous" bits.
953 | 
954 |     // For getter and setter property definitions the
955 |     // rvalue type != the property type.
956 |     Node rvalue = key.getFirstChild();
957 |     JSType rightType = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(
958 |         key, getJSType(rvalue));
959 |     if (rightType == null) {
960 |       rightType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
961 |     }
962 | 
963 |     Node owner = objlit;
964 | 
965 |     // Validate value is assignable to the key type.
966 | 
967 |     JSType keyType = getJSType(key);
968 | 
969 |     JSType allowedValueType = keyType;
970 |     if (allowedValueType.isEnumElementType()) {
971 |       allowedValueType =
972 |           allowedValueType.toMaybeEnumElementType().getPrimitiveType();
973 |     }
974 | 
975 |     boolean valid = validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(t, key,
976 |         rightType, allowedValueType,
977 |         owner, NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(key));
978 |     if (valid) {
979 |       ensureTyped(t, key, rightType);
980 |     } else {
981 |       ensureTyped(t, key);
982 |     }
983 | 
984 |     // Validate that the key type is assignable to the object property type.
985 |     // This is necessary as the objlit may have been cast to a non-literal
986 |     // object type.
987 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider introducing a CAST node to the AST (or
988 |     // perhaps a parentheses node).
989 | 
990 |     JSType objlitType = getJSType(objlit);
991 |     ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
992 |         objlitType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
993 |     if (type != null) {
994 |       String property = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(key);
995 |       if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
996 |           !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
997 |           !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
998 |         validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
999 |             t, key, keyType,
1000 |             type.getPropertyType(property), owner, property);
1001 |       }
1002 |       return;
1003 |     }
1004 |   }

===== M0396 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 637-664
Documentation:

Method code:
637 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
638 |     Node lastJump = null;
639 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
640 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
641 |       if (curHandler.isFunction()) {
642 |         break;
643 |       }
644 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
645 |         if (lastJump == null) {
646 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
647 |         } else {
648 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
649 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
650 |         }
651 |         lastJump = curHandler;
652 |       }
653 |     }
654 | 
655 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
656 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
657 |     }
658 | 
659 |     if (lastJump == null) {
660 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
661 |     } else {
662 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
663 |     }
664 |   }

===== M0406 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 933-975
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
933 |   @Override
934 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
935 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
936 |       return true;
937 |     }
938 | 
939 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
940 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
941 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
942 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
943 |         return true;
944 |       }
945 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
946 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
947 |         return false;
948 |       }
949 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
950 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
951 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
952 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
953 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
954 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
955 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
956 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
957 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
958 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
959 | 
960 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
961 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
962 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
963 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
964 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
965 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
966 | 
967 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
968 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
969 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
970 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
971 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
972 |     }
973 | 
974 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
975 |   }

===== M0415 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0437 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.isTry() &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

===== M0441 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 92-189
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   @Override
93 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
94 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
95 | 
96 |     switch (n.getType()) {
97 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
98 |       case Token.NAME:
99 |         if (parent == null) {
100 |           return;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
104 |         if (!parent.isVar() &&
105 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
106 |             !(parent.isAssign() &&
107 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
108 |           return;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
112 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
113 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
114 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
115 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
116 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
117 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
118 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
119 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
120 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
121 |         if (docInfo == null &&
122 |             !(parent.isVar() &&
123 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
124 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
128 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
129 |         if (varType == null && parent.isFunction()) {
130 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
134 |         // we can do.
135 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
140 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
141 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
142 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
147 |         break;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
150 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
151 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
152 |         //
153 |         // 1)
154 |         // /** @deprecated */
155 |         // obj.prop = ...
156 |         //
157 |         // 2)
158 |         // /** @deprecated */
159 |         // obj.prop;
160 |         if (parent.isExprResult() ||
161 |             (parent.isAssign() &&
162 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
163 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
164 |           if (docInfo == null) {
165 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
166 |           }
167 |           if (docInfo != null) {
168 |             ObjectType lhsType =
169 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
170 |             if (lhsType != null) {
171 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
172 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
173 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
174 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo);
175 |               }
176 | 
177 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
178 |               ObjectType propType =
179 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
180 |               if (propType != null) {
181 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
182 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
183 |               }
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |         break;
188 |     }
189 |   }

===== M0451 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *

   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1386 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1387 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1388 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1389 |     // merge to fix this.
1390 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1391 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1392 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1393 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1394 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1395 | 
1396 |       if (value != null) {
1397 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1398 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1399 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1400 | 
1401 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1402 |         if (info == null) {
1403 |           info = varInfo;
1404 |         }
1405 | 
1406 |         checkEnumAlias(t, info, value);
1407 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1408 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1409 |         } else {
1410 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1411 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1412 |         }
1413 |       }
1414 |     }
1415 |   }

===== M0464 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.registerMismatch(JSType,JSType,JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 641-665
Documentation:

Method code:
641 |   private void registerMismatch(JSType found, JSType required, JSError error) {
642 |     // Don't register a mismatch for differences in null or undefined or if the
643 |     // code didn't downcast.
644 |     found = found.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
645 |     required = required.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
646 |     if (found.canAssignTo(required) || required.canAssignTo(found)) {
647 |       return;
648 |     }
649 | 
650 |     mismatches.add(new TypeMismatch(found, required, error));
651 |     if (found.isFunctionType() &&
652 |         required.isFunctionType()) {
653 |       FunctionType fnTypeA = found.toMaybeFunctionType();
654 |       FunctionType fnTypeB = required.toMaybeFunctionType();
655 |       Iterator<Node> paramItA = fnTypeA.getParameters().iterator();
656 |       Iterator<Node> paramItB = fnTypeB.getParameters().iterator();
657 |       while (paramItA.hasNext() && paramItB.hasNext()) {
658 |         registerIfMismatch(paramItA.next().getJSType(),
659 |             paramItB.next().getJSType(), error);
660 |       }
661 | 
662 |       registerIfMismatch(
663 |         fnTypeA.getReturnType(), fnTypeB.getReturnType(), error);
664 |     }
665 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1706-1732
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1706 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1707 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1708 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1709 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1710 |       skipEOLs();
1711 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1712 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1713 |       skipEOLs();
1714 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1715 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1716 |       skipEOLs();
1717 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1718 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1719 |       String string = stream.getString();
1720 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1721 |         skipEOLs();
1722 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1723 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1724 |         return newStringNode(string);
1725 |       } else {
1726 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1727 |       }
1728 |     }
1729 | 
1730 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1731 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1732 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseFieldTypeList(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2081-2107
Documentation:
/**
   * FieldTypeList := FieldType | FieldType ',' FieldTypeList
   */
Method code:
2081 |   private Node parseFieldTypeList(JsDocToken token) {
2082 |     Node fieldTypeList = newNode(Token.LB);
2083 | 
2084 |     do {
2085 |       Node fieldType = parseFieldType(token);
2086 | 
2087 |       if (fieldType == null) {
2088 |         return null;
2089 |       }
2090 | 
2091 |       fieldTypeList.addChildToBack(fieldType);
2092 | 
2093 |       skipEOLs();
2094 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.COMMA)) {
2095 |         break;
2096 |       }
2097 | 
2098 |       // Move to the comma token.
2099 |       next();
2100 | 
2101 |       // Move to the token passed the comma.
2102 |       skipEOLs();
2103 |       token = next();
2104 |     } while (true);
2105 | 
2106 |     return fieldTypeList;
2107 |   }

===== M0502 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.findOverriddenFunction(ObjectType,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 950-970
Documentation:
/**
     * Find the function that's being overridden on this type, if any.
     */
Method code:
950 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
951 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName) {
952 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
953 |       // on a superclass.
954 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);
955 |       if (propType != null && propType.isFunctionType()) {
956 |         return propType.toMaybeFunctionType();
957 |       } else {
958 |         // If it's not, then check to see if it's implemented
959 |         // on an implemented interface.
960 |         for (ObjectType iface :
961 |                  ownerType.getCtorImplementedInterfaces()) {
962 |           propType = iface.getPropertyType(propName);
963 |           if (propType != null && propType.isFunctionType()) {
964 |             return propType.toMaybeFunctionType();
965 |           }
966 |         }
967 |       }
968 | 
969 |       return null;
970 |     }

===== M0504 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.isQualifiedNameInferred(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1588-1613
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a qualified name is inferred.
     * NOTE(nicksantos): Determining whether a property is declared or not
     * is really really obnoxious.
     *
     * The problem is that there are two (equally valid) coding styles:
     *
     * (function() {
     *   /* The authoritative definition of goog.bar. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * })();
     *
     * function f() {
     *   goog.bar();
     *   /* Reset goog.bar to a no-op. /
     *   goog.bar = function() {};
     * }
     *
     * In a dynamic language with first-class functions, it's very difficult
     * to know which one the user intended without looking at lots of
     * contextual information (the second example demonstrates a small case
     * of this, but there are some really pathological cases as well).
     *
     * The current algorithm checks if either the declaration has
     * jsdoc type information, or @const with a known type,
     * or a function literal with a name we haven't seen before.
     */
Method code:
1588 |     private boolean isQualifiedNameInferred(
1589 |         String qName, Node n, JSDocInfo info,
1590 |         Node rhsValue, JSType valueType) {
1591 |       if (valueType == null) {
1592 |         return true;
1593 |       }
1594 | 
1595 |       boolean inferred = true;
1596 |       if (info != null) {
1597 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1598 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1599 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1600 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1601 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1602 |       }
1603 | 
1604 |       if (inferred && rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isFunction()) {
1605 |         if (info != null) {
1606 |           inferred = false;
1607 |         } else if (!scope.isDeclared(qName, false) &&
1608 |                    n.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
1609 |           inferred = false;
1610 |         }
1611 |       }
1612 |       return inferred;
1613 |     }

===== M0512 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0526 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0528 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 811-835
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
811 |   @Override
812 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
813 |     FunctionType that =
814 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(otherType);
815 |     if (that == null) {
816 |       return false;
817 |     }
818 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
819 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
820 |         return this == that;
821 |       }
822 |       return false;
823 |     }
824 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
825 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
826 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
827 |       }
828 |       return false;
829 |     }
830 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
831 |       return false;
832 |     }
833 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
834 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
835 |   }

===== M0549 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,CompilerInput,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 502-562
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   * @return The variable we end up with. Most of the time, this will just
   *     be {@code var}, but in some rare cases we will need to declare
   *     a new var with new source info.
   */
Method code:
502 |   Var expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, CompilerInput input,
503 |       Node n, Node parent, Var var, String variableName, JSType newType) {
504 |     Var newVar = var;
505 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
506 |     if (n.isGetProp() ||
507 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
508 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
509 |       if (info == null) {
510 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
511 |       }
512 |       allowDupe =
513 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
514 |     }
515 | 
516 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
517 | 
518 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
519 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
520 |     // compilation process.
521 |     if (varType != null &&
522 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
523 |         newType != null &&
524 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
525 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
526 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
527 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
528 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
529 |       // native type. We should redeclare it at the new input site.
530 |       if (var.input == null) {
531 |         Scope s = var.getScope();
532 |         s.undeclare(var);
533 |         newVar = s.declare(variableName, n, varType, input, false);
534 | 
535 |         n.setJSType(varType);
536 |         if (parent.isVar()) {
537 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
538 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
539 |           }
540 |         } else {
541 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
542 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
543 |         }
544 |       } else {
545 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
546 |         // match the original type.
547 |         //
548 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
549 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
550 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
551 |               var.getParentNode().isExprResult()) ||
552 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
553 |           report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
554 |               variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
555 |               String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
556 |               varType.toString()));
557 |         }
558 |       }
559 |     }
560 | 
561 |     return newVar;
562 |   }

/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.

Method code:
374 |   boolean expectCanAssignTo(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
375 |       JSType leftType, String msg) {
376 |     if (!rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {

379 |         // the line numbers of both types.

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.allFlowSlots()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 344-360
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets all the symbols that have been defined before this point
   * in the current flow. Does not return slots that have not changed during
   * the flow.
   *
   * For example, consider the code:
   * <code>
   * var x = 3;
   * function f() {
   *   var y = 5;
   *   y = 6; // FLOW POINT
   *   var z = y;
   *   return z;
   * }
   * </code>
   * A FlowScope at FLOW POINT will return a slot for y, but not
   * a slot for x or z.
   */
Method code:
344 |   private Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> allFlowSlots() {
345 |     Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slots = Maps.newHashMap();
346 |     for (LinkedFlowSlot slot = lastSlot;
347 |          slot != null; slot = slot.parent) {
348 |       if (!slots.containsKey(slot.getName())) {
349 |         slots.put(slot.getName(), slot);
350 |       }
351 |     }
352 |

354 |       if (!slots.containsKey(symbolEntry.getKey())) {
355 |         slots.put(symbolEntry.getKey(), symbolEntry.getValue());
356 |       }
357 |     }
358 | 
359 |     return slots;
360 |   }

/**
   * FieldType := FieldName | FieldName ':' TypeExpression
   */
Method code:
2112 |   private Node parseFieldType(JsDocToken token) {
2113 |     Node fieldName = parseFieldName(token);
2114 | 
2115 |     if (fieldName == null) {
2116 |       return null;
2117 |     }
2118 | 
2119 |     skipEOLs();
2120 |     if (!match(JsDocToken.COLON)) {
2121 |       return fieldName;
2122 |     }
2123 | 
2124 |     // Move to the colon.
2125 |     next();
2126 | 
2127 |     // Move to the token after the colon and parse
2128 |     // the type expression.
2129 |     skipEOLs();
2130 |     Node typeExpression = parseTypeExpression(next());
2131 |

2133 |       return null;
2134 |     }
2135 |

===== M0576 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1739-1774
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1739 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1740 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1741 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1742 |     }
1743 | 
1744 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1745 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1746 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1747 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1748 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1749 |       skipEOLs();
1750 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1751 |         next();
1752 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1753 |       }
1754 |     }
1755 | 
1756 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1757 | 
1758 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1759 |       next();
1760 |       skipEOLs();
1761 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1762 |       if (memberType != null) {
1763 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1764 | 
1765 |         skipEOLs();
1766 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1767 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1768 |         }
1769 | 
1770 |         next();
1771 |       }
1772 |     }
1773 |     return typeNameNode;
1774 |   }

===== M0583 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 828-855
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
828 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
829 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
830 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
831 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
832 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
833 |           return;
834 |         }
835 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
836 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
837 | 
838 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
839 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
840 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
841 |           } else {
842 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           }
844 |         } else { // Has a catch.
845 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
846 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
847 |             return;
848 |           } else {
849 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
850 |           }
851 |         }
852 |         lastJump = handler;
853 |       }
854 |     }
855 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1452-1606
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1452 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1453 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1454 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1455 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1456 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1457 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1458 | 
1459 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1460 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1461 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1462 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1463 | 
1464 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1465 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1466 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1467 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1468 |         }
1469 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1470 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1471 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1472 | 
1473 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1474 |         return createOptionalType(
1475 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1476 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1477 | 
1478 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1479 |         return createOptionalType(
1480 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1481 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1482 | 
1483 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1484 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1485 | 
1486 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1487 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1488 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1489 | 
1490 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1491 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1492 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1493 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1494 |           builder.addAlternate(
1495 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1496 |         }
1497 |         return builder.build();
1498 | 
1499 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1500 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1501 | 
1502 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1503 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1504 | 
1505 |       case Token.STRING:
1506 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1507 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1508 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1509 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1510 |         }
1511 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1512 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1513 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1514 |           if (typeList != null &&
1515 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1516 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1517 |             JSType parameterType =
1518 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1519 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1520 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1521 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1522 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1523 |               JSType indexType =
1524 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1525 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1526 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1527 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1528 |             }
1529 |           }
1530 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1531 |         } else {
1532 |           return namedType;
1533 |         }
1534 | 
1535 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1536 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1537 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1538 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1539 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1540 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1541 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1542 |           thisType =
1543 |               ObjectType.cast(
1544 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1545 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1546 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1547 |           if (thisType == null) {
1548 |             reporter.warning(
1549 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1550 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1551 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1552 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1553 |                 sourceName,
1554 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1555 |           }
1556 | 
1557 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1558 |           current = current.getNext();
1559 |         }
1560 | 
1561 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1562 | 
1563 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1564 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1565 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1566 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1567 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1568 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1569 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1570 |               } else {
1571 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1572 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1573 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1574 |               }
1575 |             } else {
1576 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1577 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1578 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1579 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1580 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1581 |                   reporter.warning(
1582 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1583 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1584 |                 }
1585 |               } else {
1586 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1587 |               }
1588 |             }
1589 |           }
1590 |           current = current.getNext();
1591 |         }
1592 | 
1593 |         JSType returnType =
1594 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1595 | 
1596 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1597 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1598 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1599 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1600 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1601 |             .build();
1602 |     }
1603 | 
1604 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1605 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1606 |   }

441 |     @Override
442 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
443 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
444 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
445 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
446 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
447 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
448 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {

===== M0595 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.getTypeIfRefinable(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ChainableReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 116-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns the type of a node in the given scope if the node corresponds to a
   * name whose type is capable of being refined.
   * @return The current type of the node if it can be refined, null otherwise.
   */
Method code:
116 |   JSType getTypeIfRefinable(Node node, FlowScope scope) {
117 |     switch (node.getType()) {
118 |       case Token.NAME:
119 |         StaticSlot<JSType> nameVar = scope.getSlot(node.getString());
120 |         if (nameVar != null) {
121 |           JSType nameVarType = nameVar.getType();
122 |           if (nameVarType == null) {
123 |             nameVarType = node.getJSType();
124 |           }
125 |           return nameVarType;
126 |         }
127 |         return null;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.GETPROP:
130 |         String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
131 |         if (qualifiedName == null) {
132 |           return null;
133 |         }
134 |         StaticSlot<JSType> propVar = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
135 |         JSType propVarType = null;
136 |         if (propVar != null) {
137 |           propVarType = propVar.getType();
138 |         }
139 |         if (propVarType == null) {
140 |           propVarType = node.getJSType();
141 |         }
142 |         if (propVarType == null) {
143 |           propVarType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
144 |         }
145 |         return propVarType;
146 |     }
147 |     return null;
148 |   }

===== M0597 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 598-637
Documentation:

Method code:
598 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
599 |     String varName = n.getString();
600 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
601 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
602 |     if (value != null) {
603 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
604 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
605 |           getJSType(value));
606 |       return scope;
607 |     } else {
608 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
609 |       if (var != null) {
610 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
611 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
612 | 
613 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
614 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
615 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
616 |         boolean unflowable = isInferred &&
617 |             isUnflowable(syntacticScope.getVar(varName));
618 | 
619 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
620 |         // inferred variable.
621 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
622 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
623 |             isInferred &&
624 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
625 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
626 | 
627 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
628 |           type = var.getType();
629 |           if (type == null) {
630 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
631 |           }
632 |         }
633 |       }
634 |     }
635 |     n.setJSType(type);
636 |     return scope;
637 |   }

===== M0598 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(Node,JSType)

Original range: 2072-2098
Documentation:
/**
   * @param key A OBJECTLIT key node.
   * @return The type expected when using the key.
   */
Method code:
2072 |   static JSType getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(Node key, JSType valueType) {
2073 |     if (valueType != null) {
2074 |       switch (key.getType()) {
2075 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
2076 |           // GET must always return a function type.
2077 |           if (valueType.isFunctionType()) {
2078 |             FunctionType fntype = valueType.toMaybeFunctionType();
2079 |             valueType = fntype.getReturnType();
2080 |           } else {
2081 |             return null;
2082 |           }
2083 |           break;
2084 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
2085 |           if (valueType.isFunctionType()) {
2086 |             // SET must always return a function type.
2087 |             FunctionType fntype = valueType.toMaybeFunctionType();
2088 |             Node param = fntype.getParametersNode().getFirstChild();
2089 |             // SET function must always have one parameter.
2090 |             valueType = param.getJSType();
2091 |           } else {
2092 |             return null;
2093 |           }
2094 |           break;
2095 |       }
2096 |     }
2097 |     return valueType;
2098 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *

   *     length argument.

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)

Method code:
249 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
250 |     switch (n.getType()) {
251 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
252 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
253 |         break;
254 | 
255 |       case Token.NAME:
256 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
257 |         break;
258 | 
259 |       case Token.GETPROP:
260 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
261 |         break;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.AND:
264 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
265 |             .createChildFlowScope();
266 |         break;
267 | 
268 |       case Token.OR:
269 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
270 |             .createChildFlowScope();
271 |         break;
272 | 
273 |       case Token.HOOK:
274 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
275 |         break;
276 | 
277 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
278 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
279 |         break;
280 | 
281 |       case Token.CALL:
282 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.NEW:
286 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
290 |       case Token.ADD:
291 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
292 |         break;
293 | 
294 |       case Token.POS:
295 |       case Token.NEG:
296 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
297 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
298 |         break;
299 | 
300 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
301 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
302 |         break;
303 | 
304 |       case Token.THIS:
305 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
306 |         break;
307 | 
308 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
309 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
310 |       case Token.LSH:
311 |       case Token.RSH:
312 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
313 |       case Token.URSH:
314 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
315 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
316 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
317 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
318 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
319 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
320 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
321 |       case Token.DIV:
322 |       case Token.MOD:
323 |       case Token.BITAND:
324 |       case Token.BITXOR:
325 |       case Token.BITOR:
326 |       case Token.MUL:
327 |       case Token.SUB:
328 |       case Token.DEC:
329 |       case Token.INC:
330 |       case Token.BITNOT:
331 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
332 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
333 |         break;
334 | 
335 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
336 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
337 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
338 |         break;
339 | 
340 |       case Token.COMMA:
341 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
342 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
343 |         break;
344 | 
345 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
346 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
347 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
348 |         break;
349 | 
350 |       case Token.DELPROP:
351 |       case Token.LT:
352 |       case Token.LE:
353 |       case Token.GT:
354 |       case Token.GE:
355 |       case Token.NOT:
356 |       case Token.EQ:
357 |       case Token.NE:
358 |       case Token.SHEQ:
359 |       case Token.SHNE:
360 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
361 |       case Token.IN:
362 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
363 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
364 |         break;
365 | 
366 |       case Token.GETELEM:
367 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
368 |         break;
369 | 
370 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
371 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
372 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isGetProp()) {
373 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
374 |         }
375 |         break;
376 | 
377 |       case Token.SWITCH:
378 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
379 |         break;
380 | 
381 |       case Token.RETURN:
382 |         scope = traverseReturn(n, scope);
383 |         break;
384 | 
385 |       case Token.VAR:
386 |       case Token.THROW:
387 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
388 |         break;
389 | 
390 |       case Token.CATCH:
391 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
392 |         break;
393 |     }
394 |     if (!n.isFunction()) {
395 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
396 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
397 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
398 | 
399 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
400 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
401 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
402 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
403 |             n.getParent().isExprResult()) {
404 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
405 |         }
406 | 
407 |         n.setJSType(castType);
408 |       }
409 |     }
410 | 
411 |     return scope;
412 |   }

===== M0616 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()

Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2320 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2321 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2322 |     int c;
2323 |     while (true) {
2324 |       c = stream.getChar();
2325 |       if (c == ' ') {
2326 |         continue;
2327 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2328 |         matchedLc = true;
2329 |         break;
2330 |       } else {
2331 |         break;
2332 |       }
2333 |     }
2334 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2335 |     return matchedLc;
2336 |   }

===== M0622 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1974-2013
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a new union type, with an alternate that has already been
   * parsed. The alternate may be null.
   */
Method code:
1974 |   private Node parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken token, Node alternate) {
1975 |     Node union = newNode(Token.PIPE);
1976 |     if (alternate != null) {
1977 |       union.addChildToBack(alternate);
1978 |     }
1979 | 
1980 |     Node expr = null;
1981 |     do {
1982 |       if (expr != null) {
1983 |         skipEOLs();
1984 |         token = next();
1985 |         Preconditions.checkState(
1986 |             token == JsDocToken.PIPE || token == JsDocToken.COMMA);
1987 | 
1988 |         boolean isPipe = token == JsDocToken.PIPE;
1989 |         if (isPipe && match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1990 |           // We support double pipes for backwards compatiblity.
1991 |           next();
1992 |         }
1993 |         skipEOLs();
1994 |         token = next();
1995 |       }
1996 |       expr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1997 |       if (expr == null) {
1998 |         return null;
1999 |       }
2000 | 
2001 |       union.addChildToBack(expr);
2002 |       // We support commas for backwards compatiblity.
2003 |     } while (match(JsDocToken.PIPE, JsDocToken.COMMA));
2004 | 
2005 |     if (alternate == null) {
2006 |       skipEOLs();
2007 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
2008 |         return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rp");
2009 |       }
2010 |       next();
2011 |     }
2012 |     return union;
2013 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.defineInferredProperty(String,JSType,Node)

Original range: 292-310
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property whose type is inferred.
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the inferred definition of
   *        property that might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.
   */
Method code:
292 |   public final boolean defineInferredProperty(String propertyName,
293 |       JSType type, Node propertyNode) {
294 |     if (hasProperty(propertyName)) {
295 |       JSType originalType = getPropertyType(propertyName);
296 |       type = originalType == null ? type :
297 |           originalType.getLeastSupertype(type);
298 |     }
299 | 
300 |     boolean result = defineProperty(propertyName, type, true,
301 |         propertyNode);
302 |

305 |     // object can affect subtyping, it's slightly more efficient
306 |     // to register this after defining the property.
307 |     registry.registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, this);
308 |

===== M0674 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 833-932
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.isAssign()</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
833 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
834 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
835 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
836 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
837 | 
838 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
839 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
840 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
841 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
842 | 
843 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
844 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
845 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
846 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
847 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
848 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
849 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
850 |         }
851 |       }
852 | 
853 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
854 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
855 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
856 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
857 |             property, rvalue);
858 |         return;
859 |       }
860 | 
861 |       checkEnumAlias(t, info, rvalue);
862 | 
863 |       // object.prototype = ...;
864 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
865 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
866 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
867 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
868 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
869 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
870 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
871 |           }
872 |         } else {
873 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
874 |         }
875 |         return;
876 |       }
877 | 
878 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
879 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
880 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
881 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
882 | 
883 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
884 |           JSType jsType = getJSType(object2);
885 |           if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
886 |             FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
887 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
888 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
889 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
890 |             }
891 |           } else {
892 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
893 |           }
894 |           return;
895 |         }
896 |       }
897 | 
898 |       // object.property = ...;
899 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
900 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
901 |       if (type != null) {
902 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
903 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
904 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
905 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
906 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
907 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
908 |         }
909 |         return;
910 |       }
911 |     } else if (lvalue.isName()) {
912 |       // variable with inferred type case
913 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
914 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
915 |       if (var != null) {
916 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
917 |           return;
918 |         }
919 |       }
920 |     }
921 | 
922 |     // fall through case
923 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
924 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
925 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
926 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
927 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
928 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
929 |     } else {
930 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
931 |     }
932 |   }

===== M0676 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 199-227
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
199 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
200 |     initialize();
201 |     int step = 0;
202 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
203 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
204 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
205 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
206 |       }
207 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
208 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
209 |       joinInputs(curNode);
210 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
211 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
212 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
213 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
214 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
215 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
216 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
217 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
218 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |       }
222 |       step++;
223 |     }
224 |     if (isForward()) {
225 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
226 |     }
227 |   }

===== M0679 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 242-300
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
242 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
243 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
244 |     if (oldType == null) {
245 |       return this;
246 |     }
247 | 
248 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
249 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
250 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
251 |       // Not a function literal.
252 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
253 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
254 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
258 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
259 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
260 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
261 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
262 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
263 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
264 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
265 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
266 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
267 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
268 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
269 | 
270 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
271 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
272 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
273 | 
274 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
275 |           // arguments.
276 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
277 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
278 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
279 |           }
280 |         } else {
281 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
282 |               paramBuilder,
283 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
284 |               warnedAboutArgList,
285 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
286 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
287 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
288 |         }
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal,
292 |       // but make them optional.
293 |       while (oldParams.hasNext()) {
294 |         paramBuilder.newOptionalParameterFromNode(oldParams.next());
295 |       }
296 | 
297 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
298 |     }
299 |     return this;
300 |   }

===== M0697 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1217-1246
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1217 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1218 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1219 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1220 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1221 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1222 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1223 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1224 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1225 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1226 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1227 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1228 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1229 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1230 |       return false;
1231 |     }
1232 | 
1233 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1234 |     if (type == null) {
1235 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1236 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1237 |       if (var != null) {
1238 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1239 |         if (varType != null) {
1240 |           type = varType;
1241 |         }
1242 |       }
1243 |     }
1244 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1245 |     return true;
1246 |   }

===== M0698 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }
