Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 |     compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(acceptEs5);
126 | 
127 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
128 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
129 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 794-808
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
794 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
795 |     switch (n.getType()) {
796 |       case Token.DO:
797 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
798 |       case Token.FOR:
799 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
800 |           return n;
801 |         }
802 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
803 |       case Token.LABEL:
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
805 |       default:
806 |         return n;
807 |     }
808 |   }

===== M0714 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitReturn(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1650-1685
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a RETURN node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1650 |   private void visitReturn(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1651 |     Node function = t.getEnclosingFunction();
1652 | 
1653 |     // This is a misplaced return, but the real JS will fail to compile,
1654 |     // so let it go.
1655 |     if (function == null) {
1656 |       return;
1657 |     }
1658 |     JSType jsType = getJSType(function);
1659 | 
1660 |     if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
1661 |       FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1662 | 
1663 |       JSType returnType = functionType.getReturnType();
1664 | 
1665 |       // if no return type is specified, undefined must be returned
1666 |       // (it's a void function)
1667 |       if (returnType == null) {
1668 |         returnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1669 |       }
1670 | 
1671 |       // fetching the returned value's type
1672 |       Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
1673 |       JSType actualReturnType;
1674 |       if (valueNode == null) {
1675 |         actualReturnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1676 |         valueNode = n;
1677 |       } else {
1678 |         actualReturnType = getJSType(valueNode);
1679 |       }
1680 | 
1681 |       // verifying
1682 |       validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, valueNode, actualReturnType, returnType,
1683 |           "inconsistent return type");
1684 |     }
1685 |   }

/**
     * Process an object literal and all the types on it.
     * @param objLit The OBJECTLIT node.
     * @param objLitType The type of the OBJECTLIT node. This might be a named
     *     type, because of the lends annotation.
     * @param declareOnOwner If true, declare properties on the objLitType as
     *     well. If false, the caller should take crae of this.
     */
Method code:
660 |     void processObjectLitProperties(
661 |         Node objLit, ObjectType objLitType,
662 |         boolean declareOnOwner) {
663 |       for (Node keyNode = objLit.getFirstChild(); keyNode != null;
664 |            keyNode = keyNode.getNext()) {
665 |         Node value = keyNode.getFirstChild();
666 |         String memberName = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(keyNode);
667 |         JSDocInfo info = keyNode.getJSDocInfo();
668 |         JSType valueType =
669 |             getDeclaredType(keyNode.getSourceFileName(), info, keyNode, value);
670 |         JSType keyType =  objLitType.isEnumType() ?
671 |             objLitType.toMaybeEnumType().getElementsType() :
672 |             NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(keyNode, valueType);
673 | 
674 |         // Try to declare this property in the current scope if it
675 |         // has an authoritative name.
676 |         String qualifiedName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(keyNode);
677 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
678 |           boolean inferred = keyType == null;
679 |           defineSlot(keyNode, objLit, qualifiedName, keyType, inferred);
680 |         } else if (keyType != null) {
681 |           setDeferredType(keyNode, keyType);
682 |         }
683 | 
684 |         if (keyType != null && objLitType != null && declareOnOwner) {
685 |           // Declare this property on its object literal.
686 |           boolean isExtern = keyNode.isFromExterns();
687 |           objLitType.defineDeclaredProperty(memberName, keyType, keyNode);
688 |         }
689 |       }

===== M0719 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise

198 |       token = blockInfo.token;

200 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
201 |       }
202 |     } else {
203 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
204 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
205 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking

224 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

259 |                   }
260 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();

280 |                   }

818 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
819 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
820 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
821 |                     // it as the unknown type.
822 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
823 |                   } else {
824 |                     skipEOLs();
825 |                     token = next();
826 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
827 |                   }
828 | 
829 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
830 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
831 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
832 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
833 |                     }
834 |                   }
835 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
836 | 
837 |                   if (type == null) {
838 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
839 |                     // recovering parsing
840 |                   } else {
841 |                     switch (annotation) {
842 |                       case DEFINE:
843 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
844 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
845 |                               lineno, charno);
846 |                         }
847 |                         break;
848 | 
849 |                       case RETURN:
850 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
851 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
852 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
853 |                           break;
854 |                         }
855 | 
856 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
857 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
858 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
859 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
860 | 
861 |                           String returnDescription =
862 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
863 | 
864 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
865 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
866 |                                 returnDescription);
867 |                           }
868 | 
869 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
870 |                         } else {
871 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
872 |                         }
873 |                         continue retry;
874 | 
875 |                       case THIS:
876 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
877 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
878 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
879 |                         }
880 |                         break;
881 | 
882 |                       case TYPE:
883 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
884 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
885 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
886 |                         }
887 |                         break;
888 | 
889 |                       case TYPEDEF:
890 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
891 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
892 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
893 |                         }
894 |                         break;
895 |                     }
896 |                   }
897 | 
898 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
899 |                   continue retry;
900 |               }
901 |             }
902 |           }
903 |           break;
904 | 
905 |         case EOC:
906 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
907 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
908 |           }
909 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
910 |           return true;
911 | 
912 |         case EOF:
913 |           // discard any accumulated information
914 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
915 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
916 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
917 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
918 |           return false;
919 | 
920 |         case EOL:
921 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
922 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
923 |           }
924 |           token = next();
925 |           continue retry;
926 | 
927 |         default:
928 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
929 |             token = next();
930 |             continue retry;
931 |           } else {
932 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
933 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
934 |             continue retry;
935 |           }
936 |       }
937 | 
938 |       // next token
939 |       token = next();
940 |     }
941 |   }

/**
   * Declares a refined type in {@code scope} for the name represented by
   * {@code node}. It must be possible to refine the type of the given node in
   * the given scope, as determined by {@link #getTypeIfRefinable}.
   */
Method code:
155 |   protected void declareNameInScope(FlowScope scope, Node node, JSType type) {
156 |     switch (node.getType()) {
157 |       case Token.NAME:
158 |         scope.inferSlotType(node.getString(), type);
159 |         break;
160 | 
161 |       case Token.GETPROP:
162 |         String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
163 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(qualifiedName);
164 | 
165 |         JSType origType = node.getJSType();
166 |         origType = origType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : origType;
167 |         scope.inferQualifiedSlot(node, qualifiedName, origType, type);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       default:
171 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("Node cannot be refined. \n" +
172 |             node.toStringTree());
173 |     }
174 |   }

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
443 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
444 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
445 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
446 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
447 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {

454 |         inputs.add(input);

456 |       }

1098 |           safeResolve(prototypeSlot.getType(), t, scope));

1102 |     // is handled further upstream.
1103 |     //
1104 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Handle this correctly if we have a UnionType.
1105 |     //
1106 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the runtime coerces "null" to the global
1107 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
1108 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the
1109 |     // fact that when most people write @this {Foo}, they really don't
1110 |     // mean "nullable Foo". For certain tags (like @extends) we de-nullify
1111 |     // the name for them.

1113 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis != null) {
1114 |       maybeTypeOfThis = maybeTypeOfThis.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1115 |     }
1116 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis instanceof ObjectType) {
1117 |       typeOfThis = (ObjectType) maybeTypeOfThis;
1118 |     }
1119 |

1121 |     ImmutableList.Builder<ObjectType> resolvedInterfaces =
1122 |         ImmutableList.builder();
1123 |     for (ObjectType iface : implementedInterfaces) {
1124 |       ObjectType resolvedIface = (ObjectType) iface.resolve(t, scope);
1125 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
1126 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
1127 |     }
1128 |     if (changed) {
1129 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
1130 |     }
1131 | 
1132 |     if (subTypes != null) {
1133 |       for (int i = 0; i < subTypes.size(); i++) {
1134 |         subTypes.set(
1135 |             i, JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(subTypes.get(i).resolve(t, scope)));
1136 |       }
1137 |     }
1138 | 
1139 |     return super.resolveInternal(t, scope);
1140 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java

Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 232-295
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
232 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
233 |     this.options = options;
234 |     if (errorManager == null) {
235 |       if (outStream == null) {
236 |         setErrorManager(
237 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
238 |       } else {
239 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
240 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
241 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
242 |         setErrorManager(printer);
243 |       }
244 |     }
245 | 
246 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
247 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
248 |       options.checkTypes = true;
249 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
250 |       options.checkTypes = false;
251 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
252 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
253 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
254 |       // parser type warnings.
255 |       options.setWarningLevel(
256 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
257 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
258 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
259 |     }
260 | 
261 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
262 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
263 |       options.setWarningLevel(
264 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
265 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
266 |     }
267 | 
268 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
269 |       options.setWarningLevel(
270 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
271 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
272 |     }
273 | 
274 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
275 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
276 |     guards.add(
277 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
278 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
279 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
280 | 
281 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
282 | 
283 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
284 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
285 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
286 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
287 |     // error off.
288 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
289 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
290 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
291 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
292 |     }
293 | 
294 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
295 |   }

===== M0770 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 151-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
151 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
152 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
153 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.BLOCK:
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |       case Token.TRY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0782 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1452-1559
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1452 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1453 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1454 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1455 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1456 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1457 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1458 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1459 | 
1460 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1461 |       // 1) @type annnotation / @enum annotation
1462 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1463 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1464 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1465 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1466 |       //
1467 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1468 |       // the function has jsdoc or has not been declared before.
1469 |       //
1470 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1471 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1472 | 
1473 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1474 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1475 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1476 |         // Determining type for #5
1477 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1478 |       }
1479 | 
1480 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1481 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1482 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1483 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1484 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1485 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1486 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1487 |         if (qVar != null) {
1488 |           // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1489 |           // and they assign F.prototype to an object literal,
1490 |           // then they are responsible for making sure that the object literal's
1491 |           // implicit prototype is set up appropriately. We just obey
1492 |           // the @extends tag.
1493 |           ObjectType qVarType = ObjectType.cast(qVar.getType());
1494 |           if (qVarType != null &&
1495 |               rhsValue != null &&
1496 |               rhsValue.isObjectLit()) {
1497 |             typeRegistry.resetImplicitPrototype(
1498 |                 rhsValue.getJSType(), qVarType.getImplicitPrototype());
1499 |           } else if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1500 |             // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1501 |             // and they assign F.prototype to some arbitrary expression,
1502 |             // there's not much we can do. We just ignore the expression,
1503 |             // and hope they've annotated their code in a way to tell us
1504 |             // what props are going to be on that prototype.
1505 |             return;
1506 |           }
1507 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1508 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1509 |           }
1510 |         }
1511 |       }
1512 | 
1513 |       if (valueType == null) {
1514 |         if (parent.isExprResult()) {
1515 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1516 |               n,
1517 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1518 |               ownerName));
1519 |         }
1520 | 
1521 |         return;
1522 |       }
1523 | 
1524 |       boolean inferred = isQualifiedNameInferred(
1525 |           qName, n, info, rhsValue, valueType);
1526 |       if (!inferred) {
1527 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1528 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1529 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1530 |           // declared yet.
1531 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1532 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1533 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1534 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1535 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1536 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1537 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, n);
1538 |           }
1539 |         }
1540 | 
1541 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1542 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1543 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1544 |       } else if (rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isTrue()) {
1545 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1546 |         FunctionType ownerType =
1547 |             JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(getObjectSlot(ownerName));
1548 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1549 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ownerType.getTypeOfThis();
1550 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1551 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1552 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1553 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1554 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1555 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE), true);
1556 |           }
1557 |         }
1558 |       }
1559 |     }

===== M0783 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.FunctionTypeBuilder(String,AbstractCompiler,Node,String,Scope)

Documentation:
/**
   * @param fnName The function name.
   * @param compiler The compiler.
   * @param errorRoot The node to associate with any warning generated by
   *     this builder.
   * @param sourceName A source name for associating any warnings that
   *     we have to emit.

/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1552 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1553 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1554 |       return getString();

1556 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1557 |       if (left == null) {
1558 |         return null;
1559 |       }
1560 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1561 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1562 |       return "this";
1563 |     } else {
1564 |       return null;
1565 |     }
1566 |   }

===== M0802 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 773-810
Documentation:

Method code:
773 |     @Override
774 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
775 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
776 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
777 |       }
778 | 
779 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
780 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
781 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
782 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
783 |             reportGetter(el);
784 |             continue;
785 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
786 |             reportSetter(el);
787 |             continue;
788 |           }
789 |         }
790 | 
791 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
792 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
793 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
794 |           key.setType(Token.GETTER_DEF);
795 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
796 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
797 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
798 |           }
799 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
800 |           key.setType(Token.SETTER_DEF);
801 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
802 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
803 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
804 |           }
805 |         }
806 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
807 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
808 |       }
809 |       return node;
810 |     }

===== M0808 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

===== M0809 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

   * @param item The item to inspect

Method code:
781 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
782 |     if (item == null) {

791 |         return item;
792 |       }
793 |     }

/**
   * Creates a RecordType from the nodes representing said record type.
   * @param n The node with type info.
   * @param sourceName The source file name.
   * @param scope A scope for doing type name lookups.
   */
Method code:
1614 |   private JSType createRecordTypeFromNodes(Node n, String sourceName,
1615 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1616 | 
1617 |     RecordTypeBuilder builder = new RecordTypeBuilder(this);
1618 | 
1619 |     // For each of the fields in the record type.
1620 |     for (Node fieldTypeNode = n.getFirstChild();
1621 |          fieldTypeNode != null;
1622 |          fieldTypeNode = fieldTypeNode.getNext()) {
1623 | 
1624 |       // Get the property's name.
1625 |       Node fieldNameNode = fieldTypeNode;
1626 |       boolean hasType = false;
1627 | 
1628 |       if (fieldTypeNode.getType() == Token.COLON) {
1629 |         fieldNameNode = fieldTypeNode.getFirstChild();
1630 |         hasType = true;
1631 |       }
1632 | 
1633 |       String fieldName = fieldNameNode.getString();
1634 | 
1635 |       // TODO(user): Move this into the lexer/parser.
1636 |       // Remove the string literal characters around a field name,
1637 |       // if any.
1638 |       if (fieldName.startsWith("'") || fieldName.startsWith("\"")) {
1639 |         fieldName = fieldName.substring(1, fieldName.length() - 1);
1640 |       }
1641 | 
1642 |       // Get the property's type.
1643 |       JSType fieldType = null;
1644 | 
1645 |       if (hasType) {
1646 |         // We have a declared type.
1647 |         fieldType = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1648 |             fieldTypeNode.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1649 |       } else {
1650 |         // Otherwise, the type is UNKNOWN.
1651 |         fieldType = getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1652 |       }
1653 | 
1654 |       // Add the property to the record.
1655 |       if (builder.addProperty(fieldName, fieldType, fieldNameNode) == null) {
1656 |         // Duplicate field name, warning and skip
1657 |         reporter.warning(
1658 |             "Duplicate record field " + fieldName,
1659 |             sourceName,
1660 |             n.getLineno(), fieldNameNode.getCharno());
1661 |       }
1662 |     }
1663 | 
1664 |     return builder.build();
1665 |   }

/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
460 |   @Override
461 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
462 |     JSType childType;
463 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
464 |     Node left, right;
465 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
466 |     boolean typeable = true;
467 | 
468 |     switch (n.getType()) {
469 |       case Token.NAME:
470 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
474 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
475 |         // ignored here.
476 |         if (!parent.isFunction()) {
477 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
478 |         } else {
479 |           typeable = false;
480 |         }
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.COMMA:
484 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
485 |         break;
486 | 
487 |       case Token.TRUE:
488 |       case Token.FALSE:
489 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.THIS:
493 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
494 |         break;
495 | 
496 |       case Token.NULL:
497 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.NUMBER:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.STRING:
505 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
506 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
507 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
508 |         } else {
509 |           // Object literal keys are not typeable
510 |           typeable = false;
511 |         }
512 |         break;
513 | 
514 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
515 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
516 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
517 |         break;
518 | 
519 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
520 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.REGEXP:
524 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
525 |         break;
526 | 
527 |       case Token.GETPROP:
528 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
529 |         typeable = !(parent.isAssign() &&
530 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.GETELEM:
534 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
535 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
536 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
537 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
538 |         typeable = false;
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.VAR:
542 |         visitVar(t, n);
543 |         typeable = false;
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.NEW:
547 |         visitNew(t, n);
548 |         typeable = true;
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.CALL:
552 |         visitCall(t, n);
553 |         typeable = !parent.isExprResult();
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.RETURN:
557 |         visitReturn(t, n);
558 |         typeable = false;
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.DEC:
562 |       case Token.INC:
563 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
564 |         validator.expectNumber(
565 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
566 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.NOT:
570 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
571 |         break;
572 | 
573 |       case Token.VOID:
574 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
575 |         break;
576 | 
577 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
578 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
579 |         break;
580 | 
581 |       case Token.BITNOT:
582 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
583 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
584 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
585 |               childType.toString());
586 |         }
587 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.POS:
591 |       case Token.NEG:
592 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
593 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
594 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
595 |         break;
596 | 
597 |       case Token.EQ:
598 |       case Token.NE: {
599 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
600 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
601 | 
602 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
603 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
604 |         TernaryValue result =
605 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
606 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
607 |           if (n.isNE()) {
608 |             result = result.not();
609 |           }
610 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
611 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
612 |         }
613 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
614 |         break;
615 |       }
616 | 
617 |       case Token.SHEQ:
618 |       case Token.SHNE: {
619 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
620 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
621 | 
622 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
623 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
624 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
625 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
626 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
627 |               rightType.toString());
628 |         }
629 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
630 |         break;
631 |       }
632 | 
633 |       case Token.LT:
634 |       case Token.LE:
635 |       case Token.GT:
636 |       case Token.GE:
637 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
638 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
639 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
640 |           validator.expectNumber(
641 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
642 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
643 |           validator.expectNumber(
644 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
645 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
646 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
647 |           // OK.
648 |         } else {
649 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
650 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
651 |           // should match a string context.
652 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
653 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
654 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
655 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
656 |           message = "right side of comparison";
657 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
658 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
659 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
660 |         }
661 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
662 |         break;
663 | 
664 |       case Token.IN:
665 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
666 |         right = n.getLastChild();
667 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
668 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
669 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
670 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
671 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
672 |         break;
673 | 
674 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
675 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
676 |         right = n.getLastChild();
677 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
678 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
679 | 
680 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
681 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
682 |         validator.expectActualObject(
683 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
684 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
685 |         break;
686 | 
687 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
688 |         visitAssign(t, n);
689 |         typeable = false;
690 |         break;
691 | 
692 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
693 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
694 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
695 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
696 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
697 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
698 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
699 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
702 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
703 |       case Token.LSH:
704 |       case Token.RSH:
705 |       case Token.URSH:
706 |       case Token.DIV:
707 |       case Token.MOD:
708 |       case Token.BITOR:
709 |       case Token.BITXOR:
710 |       case Token.BITAND:
711 |       case Token.SUB:
712 |       case Token.ADD:
713 |       case Token.MUL:
714 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
715 |         break;
716 | 
717 |       case Token.DELPROP:
718 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
723 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
724 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
725 |         typeable = false;
726 |         break;
727 | 
728 |       case Token.WITH: {
729 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
730 |         childType = getJSType(child);
731 |         validator.expectObject(
732 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
733 |         typeable = false;
734 |         break;
735 |       }
736 | 
737 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
738 |         visitFunction(t, n);
739 |         break;
740 | 
741 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
742 |       case Token.LABEL:
743 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
744 |       case Token.SWITCH:
745 |       case Token.BREAK:
746 |       case Token.CATCH:
747 |       case Token.TRY:
748 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
749 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
750 |       case Token.BLOCK:
751 |       case Token.EMPTY:
752 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
753 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
754 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
755 |       case Token.THROW:
756 |         typeable = false;
757 |         break;
758 | 
759 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
760 |       case Token.DO:
761 |       case Token.FOR:
762 |       case Token.IF:
763 |       case Token.WHILE:
764 |         typeable = false;
765 |         break;
766 | 
767 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
768 |       case Token.AND:
769 |       case Token.HOOK:
770 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
771 |       case Token.OR:
772 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
773 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
774 |         } else {
775 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
776 |           if ((n.isObjectLit())
777 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
778 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
779 |           } else {
780 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
781 |           }
782 |         }
783 |         if (n.isObjectLit()) {
784 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
785 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
786 |           }
787 |         }
788 |         break;
789 | 
790 |       default:
791 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
792 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
793 |         break;
794 |     }
795 | 
796 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
797 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
798 | 
799 |     if (typeable) {
800 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
801 |     }
802 | 
803 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
804 |   }

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
210 |         return true;
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

===== M0829 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

===== M0834 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.RecordType.isSubtype(ObjectType,RecordType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/RecordType.java
Original range: 229-268
Documentation:
/** Determines if typeA is a subtype of typeB */
Method code:
229 |   static boolean isSubtype(ObjectType typeA, RecordType typeB) {
230 |     // typeA is a subtype of record type typeB iff:
231 |     // 1) typeA has all the properties declared in typeB.
232 |     // 2) And for each property of typeB,
233 |     //    2a) if the property of typeA is declared, it must be equal
234 |     //        to the type of the property of typeB,
235 |     //    2b) otherwise, it must be a subtype of the property of typeB.
236 |     //
237 |     // To figure out why this is true, consider the following pseudo-code:
238 |     // /** @type {{a: (Object,null)}} */ var x;
239 |     // /** @type {{a: !Object}} */ var y;
240 |     // var z = {a: {}};
241 |     // x.a = null;
242 |     //
243 |     // y cannot be assigned to x, because line 4 would violate y's declared
244 |     // properties. But z can be assigned to x. Even though z and y are the
245 |     // same type, the properties of z are inferred--and so an assignment
246 |     // to the property of z would not violate any restrictions on it.
247 |     for (String property : typeB.properties.keySet()) {
248 |       if (!typeA.hasProperty(property)) {
249 |         return false;
250 |       }
251 | 
252 |       JSType propA = typeA.getPropertyType(property);
253 |       JSType propB = typeB.getPropertyType(property);
254 |       if (!propA.isUnknownType() && !propB.isUnknownType()) {
255 |         if (typeA.isPropertyTypeDeclared(property)) {
256 |           if (!propA.isEquivalentTo(propB)) {
257 |             return false;
258 |           }
259 |         } else {
260 |           if (!propA.isSubtype(propB)) {
261 |             return false;
262 |           }
263 |         }
264 |       }
265 |     }
266 | 
267 |     return true;
268 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1026-1127
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1026 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1027 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1028 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1029 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1030 |     // already.
1031 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1032 |       return;
1033 |     }
1034 | 
1035 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1036 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1037 |         superClass.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1038 |     // For interface
1039 |     boolean superInterfacesHasProperty = false;
1040 |     if (ctorType.isInterface()) {
1041 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1042 |         superInterfacesHasProperty =
1043 |           superInterfacesHasProperty || interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName);
1044 |       }
1045 |     }
1046 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1047 | 
1048 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1049 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1050 |       for (JSType implementedInterface :
1051 |           ctorType.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
1052 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1053 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1054 |           continue;
1055 |         }
1056 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1057 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1058 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1059 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1060 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1061 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty || interfaceHasProperty;
1062 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && !declaredOverride &&
1063 |             interfaceHasProperty) {
1064 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1065 |           // property
1066 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1067 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1068 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1069 |         }
1070 |       }
1071 |     }
1072 | 
1073 |     if (!declaredOverride && !superClassHasProperty
1074 |         && !superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1075 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1076 |       return;
1077 |     }
1078 | 
1079 |     JSType topInstanceType = superClassHasProperty ?
1080 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1081 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1082 |         !declaredOverride && superClassHasProperty) {
1083 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1084 |       // property
1085 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1086 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1087 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1088 |     }
1089 |     if (!declaredOverride) {
1090 |       // there's no @override to check
1091 |       return;
1092 |     }
1093 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1094 |     if (superClassHasProperty) {
1095 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1096 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1097 |           superClass.getPrototype().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1098 |       if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superClassPropType)) {
1099 |         compiler.report(
1100 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1101 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1102 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1103 |       }
1104 |     } else if (superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1105 |       // there is an super interface property
1106 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1107 |         if (interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName)) {
1108 |           JSType superPropertyType =
1109 |             interfaceType.getPropertyType(propertyName);
1110 |           if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superPropertyType)) {
1111 |             topInstanceType = interfaceType.getConstructor().
1112 |                 getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName);
1113 |             compiler.report(
1114 |                 t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1115 |                     propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1116 |                     superPropertyType.toString(),
1117 |                     propertyType.toString()));
1118 |           }
1119 |         }
1120 |       }
1121 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty) {
1122 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1123 |       compiler.report(
1124 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1125 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1126 |     }
1127 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)

/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
425 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
426 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
427 |     if (argsParent == null) {
428 |       if (info == null) {
429 |         return this;
430 |       } else {
431 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
432 |       }
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // arguments
436 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
437 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
438 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
439 |     }
440 | 
441 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
442 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
443 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
444 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
445 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
446 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
447 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
448 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
449 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
450 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
451 | 
452 |       // type from JSDocInfo

454 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
455 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
456 | 
457 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
458 |         parameterType =
459 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
460 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
461 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
462 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
463 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
464 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
465 |       } else {
466 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
467 |       }
468 | 
469 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
470 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
471 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
472 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
473 |         }
474 |         foundTemplateType = true;
475 |       }
476 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
477 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
478 |           isOptionalParam,
479 |           isVarArgs);
480 | 
481 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
482 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
483 |       }
484 |     }
485 | 
486 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
487 |     if (!isVarArgs) {
488 |       while (oldParameterType != null && !isVarArgs) {
489 |         builder.newParameterFromNode(oldParameterType);
490 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
491 |       }
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
495 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
496 |     }
497 | 
498 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
499 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
500 |     }
501 | 
502 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
503 |     return this;
504 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)

/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1090 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1091 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1092 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1093 | 
1094 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1095 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1096 |           isGlobalVar &&
1097 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1098 |            parent.isFunction());
1099 | 
1100 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1101 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1102 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1103 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1104 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1105 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1106 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1107 | 
1108 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1109 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1110 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1111 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1112 |         }
1113 |       }
1114 | 
1115 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1116 |       // the extern info from the node.
1117 |       boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1118 |       Var newVar = null;
1119 | 
1120 |       // declared in closest scope?
1121 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1122 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1123 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1124 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1125 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1126 |       } else {
1127 |         if (!inferred) {
1128 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1129 |         }
1130 | 
1131 |         newVar =
1132 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1133 | 
1134 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1135 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1136 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1137 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1138 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1139 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1140 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1141 |           }
1142 |         }
1143 |       }
1144 | 
1145 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1146 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1147 |       if (fnType != null &&
1148 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1149 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1150 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1151 |             !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1152 |           // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1153 |           FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1154 |           ObjectType.Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1155 | 
1156 |           // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1157 |           // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1158 |           // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1159 |           // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1160 |           // the same node.
1161 |           //
1162 |           // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1163 |           // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1164 |           prototypeSlot.setNode(n);

1167 | 
1168 |           // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1169 |           // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1170 |           // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1171 |           Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1172 |           if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1173 |             scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1174 |           }
1175 | 
1176 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1177 |               n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1178 |               /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1179 |               superClassCtor == null ||
1180 |               superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1181 |                   getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1182 | 
1183 |           // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1184 |           // it constructs itself.
1185 |           if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1186 |               !isExtern &&
1187 |               // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1188 |               // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1189 |               // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1190 |               // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1191 |               // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1192 |               // this.
1193 |               variableName.equals(
1194 |                   fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1195 |             compiler.report(
1196 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1197 |                     fnType.isConstructor() ?
1198 |                     CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1199 |                     variableName));
1200 |           }
1201 |         }
1202 |       }
1203 | 
1204 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1205 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1206 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1207 |         if (inferred) {
1208 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1209 |               type == null ?
1210 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1211 |               type,
1212 |               n);
1213 |         } else {
1214 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1215 |         }
1216 |       }
1217 | 
1218 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1219 |           && type != null
1220 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1221 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1222 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1223 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1224 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1225 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1226 |         globalThisCtor
1227 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1228 |       }
1229 |     }

===== M0863 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 256-306
Documentation:

Method code:
256 |   @Override
257 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
258 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
259 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
260 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
261 |         return true;
262 |       }
263 | 
264 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
265 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
266 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
267 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
268 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
269 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
270 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
271 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
272 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
273 |         return false;
274 |       }
275 | 
276 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
277 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
278 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
279 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
280 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
281 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
282 |             return false;
283 |           }
284 |         }
285 | 
286 |         return true;
287 |       }
288 | 
289 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
290 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
291 | 
292 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
293 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
294 |           return false;
295 |         }
296 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
297 |       }
298 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
299 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
300 |           return false;
301 |         }
302 |       }
303 |       return true;
304 |     }
305 |     return false;
306 |   }

     * Handle typedefs.

     * @param info JSDoc comments.

Method code:
1782 |     private void checkForTypedef(
1783 |         NodeTraversal t, Node candidate, JSDocInfo info) {
1784 |       if (info == null || !info.hasTypedefType()) {
1785 |         return;
1786 |       }
1787 |

1789 |       if (typedef == null) {
1790 |         return;

1808 |             getNativeType(NO_TYPE), false);
