===== M0903 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1480-1541
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1480 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1481 |     FunctionType functionType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(n.getJSType());
1482 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1483 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1484 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1485 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1486 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1487 |           (baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1488 |         compiler.report(
1489 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1490 |       } else {
1491 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1492 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1493 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1494 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1495 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1496 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1497 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1498 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1499 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1500 |               badImplementedType = true;
1501 |             }
1502 |           } else {
1503 |             badImplementedType = true;
1504 |           }
1505 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1506 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1507 |           }
1508 |         }
1509 |         // check properties
1510 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1511 |       }
1512 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1513 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1514 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1515 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1516 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1517 |           compiler.report(
1518 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1519 |         }
1520 |       }
1521 |       // Interface cannot implement any interfaces
1522 |       if (functionType.hasImplementedInterfaces()) {
1523 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
1524 |             CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1525 |       }
1526 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1527 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1528 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1529 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1530 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1531 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1532 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1533 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1534 |           currentProperties.clear();
1535 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1536 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1537 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1538 |         }
1539 |       }
1540 |     }
1541 |   }

===== M0917 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0921 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1545-1580
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1545 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1546 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1547 | 
1548 |     skipEOLs();
1549 | 
1550 |     boolean restArg = false;
1551 |     token = next();
1552 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1553 |       token = next();
1554 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1555 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1556 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, IR.empty());
1557 |       }
1558 |       restArg = true;
1559 |     }
1560 | 
1561 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1562 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1563 |       skipEOLs();
1564 |       if (restArg) {
1565 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1566 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1567 |         next();
1568 |         skipEOLs();
1569 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1570 |       }
1571 | 
1572 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1573 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1574 |       } else {
1575 |         next();
1576 |       }
1577 |     }
1578 | 
1579 |     return typeNode;
1580 |   }

===== M0923 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 135-247
Documentation:

Method code:
135 |   @Override
136 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
137 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
138 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
139 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
140 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
141 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
142 |     // worthwhile.
143 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
144 |     Node condition = null;
145 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
146 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
147 | 
148 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
149 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
150 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
151 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
152 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
153 | 
154 |       switch (branch) {
155 |         case ON_TRUE:
156 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
157 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
158 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
159 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
160 | 
161 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
162 | 
163 |             if (item.isVar()) {
164 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
165 |             }
166 |             if (item.isName()) {
167 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
168 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
169 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
170 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
171 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
172 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
173 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
174 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
175 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
176 |                 }
177 |               }
178 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
179 |             }
180 |             newScope = informed;
181 |             break;
182 |           }
183 | 
184 |           // FALL THROUGH
185 | 
186 |         case ON_FALSE:
187 |           if (condition == null) {
188 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
189 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
190 |               condition = source;
191 | 
192 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
193 |               // of the loop.
194 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
195 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
196 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
197 |               }
198 |             }
199 |           }
200 | 
201 |           if (condition != null) {
202 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
203 |                 condition.isOr()) {
204 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
205 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
206 |               // scope on false.
207 |               //
208 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
209 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
210 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
211 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
212 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
213 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
214 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
215 | 
216 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
217 |               // of the loop.
218 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
219 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
220 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
221 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
222 |               }
223 |               newScope =
224 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
225 |                       condition,
226 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
227 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
228 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
229 |             } else {
230 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
231 |               // of the loop.
232 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
233 |                 conditionFlowScope =
234 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
235 |               }
236 |               newScope =
237 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
238 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
239 |             }
240 |           }
241 |           break;
242 |       }
243 | 
244 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
245 |     }
246 |     return result;
247 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */

1109 |

1113 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1114 | 
1115 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno,
1116 |           endLineno, endCharno, true);
1117 |     }

===== M0938 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseObjectLiteral(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 646-705
Documentation:

Method code:
646 |   private FlowScope traverseObjectLiteral(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
647 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
648 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(type);
649 | 
650 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null; name = name.getNext()) {
651 |       scope = traverse(name.getFirstChild(), scope);
652 |     }
653 | 
654 |     // Object literals can be reflected on other types, or changed with
655 |     // type casts.
656 |     // See CodingConvention#getObjectLiteralCase and goog.object.reflect.
657 |     // Ignore these types of literals.
658 |     // TODO(nicksantos): There should be an "anonymous object" type that
659 |     // we can check for here.
660 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(type);
661 |     if (objectType == null) {
662 |       return scope;
663 |     }
664 | 
665 |     boolean hasLendsName = n.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
666 |         n.getJSDocInfo().getLendsName() != null;
667 |     if (objectType.hasReferenceName() && !hasLendsName) {
668 |       return scope;
669 |     }
670 | 
671 |     String qObjName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(
672 |         NodeUtil.getBestLValue(n));
673 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null;
674 |          name = name.getNext()) {
675 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
676 |       String memberName = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyName(name);
677 |       if (memberName != null) {
678 |         JSType rawValueType =  name.getFirstChild().getJSType();
679 |         JSType valueType = NodeUtil.getObjectLitKeyTypeFromValueType(
680 |             name, rawValueType);
681 |         if (valueType == null) {
682 |           valueType = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
683 |         }
684 |         objectType.defineInferredProperty(memberName, valueType, name);
685 | 
686 |         // Do normal flow inference if this is a direct property assignment.
687 |         if (qObjName != null && name.isString()) {
688 |           String qKeyName = qObjName + "." + memberName;
689 |           Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(qKeyName);
690 |           JSType oldType = var == null ? null : var.getType();
691 |           if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
692 |             var.setType(oldType == null ?
693 |                 valueType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(oldType));
694 |           }
695 | 
696 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(name, qKeyName,
697 |               oldType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : oldType,
698 |               valueType);
699 |         }
700 |       } else {
701 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
702 |       }
703 |     }
704 |     return scope;
705 |   }

3068 |   static String getBestLValueName(@Nullable Node lValue) {
3069 |     if (lValue == null || lValue.getParent() == null) {
3070 |       return null;
3071 |     }

3073 |       Node owner = getBestLValue(lValue.getParent());

3081 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0944 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1902-1904
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1902 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1903 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1904 |   }

===== M0952 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 177-221
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
177 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
178 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
179 |     switch (n.getType()) {
180 |       case Token.STRING:
181 |         return n.getString();
182 | 
183 |       case Token.NAME:
184 |         String name = n.getString();
185 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
186 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
187 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
188 |           return name;
189 |         }
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NUMBER:
193 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
194 | 
195 |       case Token.FALSE:
196 |         return "false";
197 | 
198 |       case Token.TRUE:
199 |         return "true";
200 | 
201 |       case Token.NULL:
202 |         return "null";
203 | 
204 |       case Token.VOID:
205 |         return "undefined";
206 | 
207 |       case Token.NOT:
208 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
209 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
210 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
211 |         }
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
215 |         return arrayToString(n);
216 | 
217 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
218 |         return "[object Object]";
219 |     }
220 |     return null;
221 |   }

===== M0962 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 241-267
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
241 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
242 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
243 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
244 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
245 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
246 | 
247 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
248 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
249 |       // if (this.x == null) {
250 |       //   this.initializeX();
251 |       //   this.x.foo();
252 |       // }
253 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
254 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
255 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
256 |       //
257 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
258 |       if (n.isGetProp() &&
259 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
260 |         return true;
261 |       }
262 | 
263 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
264 |       return false;
265 |     }
266 |     return true;
267 |   }

===== M0985 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1154-1188
Documentation:

Method code:
1154 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1155 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1156 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1157 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1158 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1159 |     if (var != null) {
1160 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1161 |       if (varType != null) {
1162 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1163 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1164 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1165 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1166 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1167 |         } else {
1168 |           return varType;
1169 |         }
1170 |       }
1171 |     }
1172 | 
1173 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1174 |     if (objType != null) {
1175 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1176 |     }
1177 | 
1178 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
1179 |         qualifiedName != null) {
1180 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1181 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1182 |       if (regType != null) {
1183 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1184 |       }
1185 |     }
1186 | 
1187 |     return propertyType;
1188 |   }

===== M0986 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N>,JoinOp<L>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 118-128
Documentation:
/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to

===== M0999 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

===== M1010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.RecordType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/RecordType.java
Original range: 95-118
Documentation:

Method code:
95 |   @Override
96 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType other) {
97 |     if (!other.isRecordType()) {
98 |       return false;
99 |     }
100 | 
101 |     // Compare properties.
102 |     RecordType otherRecord = other.toMaybeRecordType();
103 |     if (otherRecord == this) {
104 |       return true;
105 |     }
106 | 
107 |     Set<String> keySet = properties.keySet();
108 |     Map<String, JSType> otherProps = otherRecord.properties;
109 |     if (!otherProps.keySet().equals(keySet)) {
110 |       return false;
111 |     }
112 |     for (String key : keySet) {
113 |       if (!otherProps.get(key).isEquivalentTo(properties.get(key))) {
114 |         return false;
115 |       }
116 |     }
117 |     return true;
118 |   }

   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.

Method code:
591 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
592 |     if (returnType == null &&
593 |         !contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() && !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
594 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
595 |       returnTypeInferred = true;
596 |     }
597 | 
598 |     if (returnType == null) {
599 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
603 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
604 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
605 |     }
606 | 
607 |     FunctionType fnType;
608 |     if (isConstructor) {
609 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
610 |     } else if (isInterface) {
611 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
612 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode());
613 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
614 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
615 |       }
616 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
617 |     } else {

619 |           .withName(fnName)
620 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
621 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
622 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
623 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
624 |           .withTemplateName(templateTypeName)
625 |           .build();
626 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
627 |     }

630 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
631 |     }
632 | 
633 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
634 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeName();
638 |

640 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 118-281

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
190 |       case Token.COLON:
191 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
192 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
193 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
194 |       case Token.DO:
195 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
196 |       case Token.EMPTY:
197 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
198 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
199 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
200 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
201 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
202 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
203 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
204 |         } else {
205 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
206 |                                           node.getClass() +
207 |                                           " type: " +
208 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
209 |         }
210 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
211 |       case Token.FALSE:
212 |       case Token.NULL:
213 |       case Token.THIS:
214 |       case Token.TRUE:
215 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
216 |       case Token.FOR:
217 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
218 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
219 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
220 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
221 |         } else {
222 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
223 |                                           node.getClass() +
224 |                                           " type: " +
225 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
226 |         }
227 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
228 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
229 |       case Token.GETELEM:
230 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
231 |       case Token.GETPROP:
232 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
233 |       case Token.HOOK:
234 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
235 |       case Token.IF:
236 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
237 |       case Token.LABEL:
238 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
239 |       case Token.LP:
240 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
241 |       case Token.NAME:
242 |         return processName((Name) node);
243 |       case Token.NEW:
244 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NUMBER:
246 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
247 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
248 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
249 |       case Token.REGEXP:
250 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.RETURN:
252 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
253 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
254 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
255 |       case Token.STRING:
256 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
257 |       case Token.SWITCH:
258 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
259 |       case Token.THROW:
260 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
261 |       case Token.TRY:
262 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.CONST:
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
266 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
267 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
268 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
269 |         } else {
270 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.toStringHelper(boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 352-396
Documentation:

Method code:
352 |   @Override
353 |   String toStringHelper(boolean forAnnotations) {
354 |     if (hasReferenceName()) {
355 |       return getReferenceName();
356 |     } else if (prettyPrint) {
357 |       // Don't pretty print recursively.
358 |       prettyPrint = false;
359 | 
360 |       // Use a tree set so that the properties are sorted.
361 |       Set<String> propertyNames = Sets.newTreeSet();
362 |       for (ObjectType current = this;
363 |            current != null && !current.isNativeObjectType() &&
364 |                propertyNames.size() <= MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES;
365 |            current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
366 |         propertyNames.addAll(current.getOwnPropertyNames());
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
370 |       sb.append("{");
371 | 
372 |       int i = 0;
373 |       for (String property : propertyNames) {
374 |         if (i > 0) {
375 |           sb.append(", ");
376 |         }
377 | 
378 |         sb.append(property);
379 |         sb.append(": ");
380 |         sb.append(getPropertyType(property).toStringHelper(forAnnotations));
381 | 
382 |         ++i;
383 |         if (!forAnnotations && i == MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES) {
384 |           sb.append(", ...");
385 |           break;
386 |         }
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       sb.append("}");
390 | 
391 |       prettyPrint = true;
392 |       return sb.toString();
393 |     } else {
394 |       return forAnnotations ? "?" : "{...}";
395 |     }
396 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkEnumAlias(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1768-1785
Documentation:
/**
   * <p>Checks enum aliases.
   *
   * <p>We verify that the enum element type of the enum used
   * for initialization is a subtype of the enum element type of
   * the enum the value is being copied in.</p>
   *
   * <p>Example:</p>
   * <pre>var myEnum = myOtherEnum;</pre>
   *
   * <p>Enum aliases are irregular, so we need special code for this :(</p>
   *
   * @param value the value used for initialization of the enum
   */
Method code:
1768 |   private void checkEnumAlias(
1769 |       NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo declInfo, Node value) {
1770 |     if (declInfo == null || !declInfo.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1771 |       return;
1772 |     }
1773 | 
1774 |     JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1775 |     if (!valueType.isEnumType()) {
1776 |       return;
1777 |     }
1778 | 
1779 |     EnumType valueEnumType = valueType.toMaybeEnumType();
1780 |     JSType valueEnumPrimitiveType =
1781 |         valueEnumType.getElementsType().getPrimitiveType();
1782 |     validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, value, valueEnumPrimitiveType,
1783 |         declInfo.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry),
1784 |         "incompatible enum element types");
1785 |   }

===== M1043 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 595-609
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
602 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
603 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
604 |       scopes.push(scope);
605 |     }
606 |     scopeRoots.clear();
607 | 
608 |     return scope;
609 |   }

===== M1045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }
